Schedule B line 2 is the same total over the same payers (Schedule B (2025), lines 1–4), so the two agree within scope.

Second transcription's note: The provided line 16 instructions are truncated before the list of methods. The method is chosen from the Schedule D Tax Worksheet heading. The Schedule D Tax Worksheet applies when Schedule D line 18 or 19 is more than zero with lines 15 and 16 both gains, or when Form 4952 is filed with an amount on line 4g. Neither worksheet is used when line 15 is zero or less, or when the smaller of Schedule D lines 15/16 is zero or less and there are no qualified dividends; in those cases the Tax Table/Tax Computation Worksheet is used on line 15. Otherwise the Qualified Dividends and Capital Gain Tax Worksheet is used. The test 'line 15 or line 16 of Schedule D is zero or less' is read as sd.net_gain_raw <= 0. The other line 16 taxes (Forms 8814 and 4972, section 962, recapture, 1291, 8978) are screened by scope.other_line16.

Ruling: scope and representation; project retained. The second transcription adds Form 8814, Form 4972 and the box 3 tax as inputs; the project refuses them through `scope.other_line16` (spec 2.2), so they are zero within scope. The second transcription's selection among the Tax Table, the Tax Computation Worksheet, the QDCG worksheet and the Schedule D Tax Worksheet used a guard form outside the grammar (recorded as unknown by the comparison) and so cannot be compared here; the selection itself is checked line by line in the qdcg and sdtw logs and by the engine differential.

Second transcription's note: The form does not state the amount when the filer checks line 27c to decline the EIC. 'If you do not want to claim the EIC' is read as entering no credit on line 27a.

Ruling: scope; project retained. The second adds the filer's choice not to claim the EIC as an input; the tool does not ask it (the line 27c label says so) and enters the credit whenever Steps 1 to 5 pass.

### 27b — Clergy filing Schedule SE (see instructions)
Status: **AGREE**

### 27c — If you do not want to claim the EIC, check here
Status: **DISAGREE**

Second transcription's note: The instructions say to check the box if you do not want to claim the EIC or if the line 27a instructions tell you to check it. Both conditions are encoded.

Ruling: scope; project retained. As line 27a: the choice not to claim is not asked; the instructed checks agree in substance (see eic.check_27c).

### Dependents (separated spouses) — Check if your filing status is MFS or HOH and you lived apart from your spouse for the las
Status: **AGREE**

### 28 — Additional child tax credit (ACTC) from Schedule 8812
Status: **AGREE**

### 29 — American opportunity credit from Form 8863, line 8
Status: **AGREE**

### 30 — Refundable adoption credit from Form 8839, line 13
Status: **AGREE**

### 31 — Amount from Schedule 3, line 15
Status: **AGREE**

### 32 — Add lines 27a, 28, 29, 30, and 31. These are your total other payments and refundable cred
Status: **AGREE**

### 33 — Add lines 25d, 26, and 32. These are your total payments
Status: **AGREE**

### 34 — If line 33 is more than line 24, subtract line 24 from line 33. This is the amount you ove
Status: **AGREE**

### 37 — Subtract line 33 from line 24. This is the amount you owe
Status: **AGREE**

## Refusal gates listed by the second transcription

Reported, not judged: the project often refuses before a worksheet is used, on the line that chooses it, which a per-line comparison cannot see.

| line | gates listed | not read by the project's line |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1a | scope.other_income | scope.other_income |
| 1z | scope.other_income | scope.other_income |
| 2b | sb.unsupported | - |
| 3b | sb.unsupported | - |
| 8 | scope.schedule1 | - |
| 9 | sb.unsupported, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |
| 10 | scope.schedule1 | - |
| 11a | sb.unsupported, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |
| 11b | sb.unsupported, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |
| 13a | qbi.unsupported | - |
| 13b | scope.schedule1a | - |
| 14 | qbi.unsupported, scope.schedule1a | - |
| 15 | qbi.unsupported, sb.unsupported, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1, scope.schedule1a | - |
| 16 | qbi.unsupported, sb.unsupported, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16, scope.schedule1, scope.schedule1a | - |
| 18 | qbi.unsupported, sb.unsupported, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16, scope.schedule1, scope.schedule1a | - |
| 22 | qbi.unsupported, sb.unsupported, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16, scope.schedule1, scope.schedule1a | - |
| 24 | qbi.unsupported, sb.unsupported, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16, scope.schedule1, scope.schedule1a | - |
| 29 | scope.credits | - |
| 30 | scope.credits | - |
| 32 | scope.credits | - |
| 33 | scope.credits | - |
| 34 | qbi.unsupported, sb.unsupported, scope.credits, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16, scope.schedule1, scope.schedule1a | - |
| 37 | qbi.unsupported, sb.unsupported, scope.credits, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16, scope.schedule1, scope.schedule1a | - |

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: f1040.line1a
    printed: "1a"
    label: "Total amount from Form(s) W-2, box 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.box1}
    refuses: [scope.other_income]
    note: "The instructions say to enter the total of W-2 box 1, including the spouse's on a joint return. The instructions also exclude inmate wages and nonqualified deferred compensation or section 457 pensions reported in box 1. Those cases are screened by scope.other_income rather than subtracted."

  - id: f1040.line1z
    printed: "1z"
    label: "Add lines 1a through 1h"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line1a}
    refuses: [scope.other_income]
    note: "Lines 1b-1h (household wages, unreported tips, Medicaid waiver payments, dependent care and adoption benefits, Form 8919 wages, other earned income) are outside the supported slice. They are screened by scope.other_income, so the sum reduces to line 1a."

  - id: f1040.line2b
    printed: "2b"
    label: "Taxable interest"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: interest, field: interest.amount}
    refuses: [sb.unsupported]
    note: "The instructions say to enter total taxable interest income from Forms 1099-INT/1099-OID. OID, bond premium and other adjustments are screened by sb.unsupported."

  - id: f1040.line3b
    printed: "3b"
    label: "Ordinary dividends"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: sb.ordinary_dividends}
    refuses: [sb.unsupported]
    note: "The instructions say this is total ordinary dividends, shown in box 1a of Form(s) 1099-DIV. Nominee dividends are screened by sb.unsupported."

  - id: f1040.line7a
    printed: "7a"
    label: "Capital gain or (loss). Attach Schedule D if required"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: sd.to1040}

  - id: f1040.line8
    printed: "8"
    label: "Additional income from Schedule 1, line 10"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1]
    note: "Schedule 1 is outside the slice. Only the computed case (no Schedule 1 income) is encoded."

  - id: f1040.line9
    printed: "9"
    label: "Add lines 1z, 2b, 3b, 4b, 5b, 6b, 7a, and 8. This is your total income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line1z, f1040.line2b, f1040.line3b, f1040.line7a, f1040.line8]}
    refuses: [scope.other_income]
    note: "Lines 4b, 5b and 6b (IRA, pension and Social Security taxable amounts) are not computed. They are screened by scope.other_income and omitted from the sum."

  - id: f1040.line10
    printed: "10"
    label: "Adjustments to income from Schedule 1, line 26"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1]

  - id: f1040.line11a
    printed: "11a"
    label: "Subtract line 10 from line 9. This is your adjusted gross income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: f1040.line9, take: f1040.line10}

  - id: f1040.line11b
    printed: "11b"
    label: "Amount from line 11a (adjusted gross income)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11a}

  - id: f1040.line12e
    printed: "12e"
    label: "Standard deduction or itemized deductions (from Schedule A)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: sa.deduction}

  - id: f1040.line13a
    printed: "13a"
    label: "Qualified business income deduction from Form 8995 or Form 8995-A"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: qbi.deduction}
    refuses: [qbi.unsupported]

  - id: f1040.line13b
    printed: "13b"
    label: "Additional deductions from Schedule 1-A, line 38"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1a]

  - id: f1040.line14
    printed: "14"
    label: "Add lines 12e, 13a, and 13b"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line12e, f1040.line13a, f1040.line13b]}

  - id: f1040.line15
    printed: "15"
    label: "Subtract line 14 from line 11b. If zero or less, enter -0-. This is your taxable income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line11b, take: f1040.line14}

  - id: f1040.h16_line15_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 1040, line 15, is zero or less"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, le: [f1040.line15, {const: 0}]}

  - id: f1040.h16_no_preferred
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 15 or line 16 of Schedule D is zero or less and no qualified dividends on line 3a"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, le: [sd.net_gain_raw, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1040.line3a, {const: 0}]}]}

  - id: f1040.h16_plain
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Exception: use neither the Qualified Dividends and Capital Gain Tax Worksheet nor the Schedule D Tax Worksheet"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [f1040.h16_line15_zero, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1040.h16_no_preferred, {const: true}]}]}

  - id: f1040.h16_sd_1819_positive
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 18 or line 19 of Schedule D is more than zero"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, gt: [sd.line18, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, gt: [sd.line19, {const: 0}]}]}

  - id: f1040.h16_sdtw_from_sd
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 18 or line 19 of Schedule D is more than zero and lines 15 and 16 of Schedule D are gains"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f1040.h16_sd_1819_positive, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [sd.line17, {const: true}]}]}

  - id: f1040.h16_sdtw_from_4952
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You file Form 4952 and you have an amount on line 4g"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [sd.filing4952, {const: true}]}, {op: test, gt: [f4952.line4g, {const: 0}]}]}

  - id: f1040.h16_use_sdtw
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule D Tax Worksheet conditions met"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [f1040.h16_sdtw_from_sd, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1040.h16_sdtw_from_4952, {const: true}]}]}

  - id: f1040.h16_tax_plain
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Tax on line 15 using the Tax Table or Tax Computation Worksheet"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: tax_on_amount, bind: {amount: f1040.line15, status: f1040.status}, line: tax}}

  - id: f1040.line16
    printed: "16"
    label: "Tax (see instructions). Check if any from Form(s): 1 8814 2 4972 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f1040.h16_plain, {const: true}]}
          then: f1040.h16_tax_plain
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f1040.h16_plain, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1040.h16_use_sdtw, {const: true}]}]}
          then: sdtw.line47
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f1040.h16_plain, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1040.h16_use_sdtw, {const: false}]}]}
          then: qdcg.line25
    refuses: [scope.other_line16]
    note: "The provided line 16 instructions are truncated before the list of methods. The method is chosen from the Schedule D Tax Worksheet heading. The Schedule D Tax Worksheet applies when Schedule D line 18 or 19 is more than zero with lines 15 and 16 both gains, or when Form 4952 is filed with an amount on line 4g. Neither worksheet is used when line 15 is zero or less, or when the smaller of Schedule D lines 15/16 is zero or less and there are no qualified dividends; in those cases the Tax Table/Tax Computation Worksheet is used on line 15. Otherwise the Qualified Dividends and Capital Gain Tax Worksheet is used. The test 'line 15 or line 16 of Schedule D is zero or less' is read as sd.net_gain_raw <= 0. The other line 16 taxes (Forms 8814 and 4972, section 962, recapture, 1291, 8978) are screened by scope.other_line16."

  - id: f1040.line17
    printed: "17"
    label: "Amount from Schedule 2, line 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s2.line3}

  - id: f1040.line18
    printed: "18"
    label: "Add lines 16 and 17"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line16, f1040.line17]}

  - id: f1040.line19
    printed: "19"
    label: "Child tax credit or credit for other dependents from Schedule 8812"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s8812.line14}

  - id: f1040.line20
    printed: "20"
    label: "Amount from Schedule 3, line 8"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s3.line8}

  - id: f1040.line21
    printed: "21"
    label: "Add lines 19 and 20"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line19, f1040.line20]}

  - id: f1040.line22
    printed: "22"
    label: "Subtract line 21 from line 18. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line18, take: f1040.line21}

  - id: f1040.line23
    printed: "23"
    label: "Other taxes, including self-employment tax, from Schedule 2, line 21"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s2.line21}

  - id: f1040.line24
    printed: "24"
    label: "Add lines 22 and 23. This is your total tax"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line22, f1040.line23]}

  - id: f1040.line25a
    printed: "25a"
    label: "Federal income tax withheld from Form(s) W-2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.box2}

  - id: f1040.line25d
    printed: "25d"
    label: "Add lines 25a through 25c"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line25a, f1040.line25b, f1040.line25c]}

  - id: f1040.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "No amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}

  - id: f1040.h_eic_decline
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Do you not want to claim the EIC?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: input, source: "Form 1040 line 27c: If you do not want to claim the EIC, check here"}

  - id: f1040.line27a
    printed: "27a"
    label: "Earned income credit (EIC)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_eic_decline, {const: true}]}
          then: f1040.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_eic_decline, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.credit
    note: "The form does not state the amount when the filer checks line 27c to decline the EIC. 'If you do not want to claim the EIC' is read as entering no credit on line 27a."

  - id: f1040.line27b
    printed: "27b"
    label: "Clergy filing Schedule SE (see instructions)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: eic.clergy}

  - id: f1040.line27c
    printed: "27c"
    label: "If you do not want to claim the EIC, check here"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [f1040.h_eic_decline, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.check_27c, {const: true}]}]}
    note: "The instructions say to check the box if you do not want to claim the EIC or if the line 27a instructions tell you to check it. Both conditions are encoded."

  - id: "f1040.lineDependents (separated spouses)"
    printed: "Dependents (separated spouses)"
    label: "Check if your filing status is MFS or HOH and you lived apart from your spouse for the last 6 months of 2025, or you are legally separated according to your state law under a written separation agreement or a decree of separate maintenance and you did not live in the same household as your spouse at the end of 2025."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: eic.separated_box}
    note: "The EIC instructions (Step 3, question 6 and the special rule for separated spouses) direct when this box is checked. The value is taken from the EIC helper that encodes that flow."

  - id: f1040.line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Additional child tax credit (ACTC) from Schedule 8812"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s8812.line27}

  - id: f1040.line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "American opportunity credit from Form 8863, line 8"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [scope.credits]

  - id: f1040.line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "Refundable adoption credit from Form 8839, line 13"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [scope.credits]

  - id: f1040.line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Amount from Schedule 3, line 15"
    type: NonNegDollars
